# BACKFILL — P1 Langkah 1-2 (sebelum logging diaktifkan)

Logging baru diaktifkan pada 2026-11-10 sebelum P1 Langkah 3. Perintah berikut sudah
dijalankan lebih dulu tanpa pembungkus, dicatat di sini agar log tidak mulai di tengah fase.
Keluaran mentahnya hanya ada di transkrip sesi; berkas bukti yang dihasilkan ada di repo.

### (tanpa timestamp tercatat) | BACKFILL: venv + dependensi API (Langkah 1)
$ bash <<'CMD'
cd musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency/api
python3 -m venv .venv
.venv/bin/pip install -q --upgrade pip
.venv/bin/pip install -q -r requirements.txt
.venv/bin/pip list | grep -iE "fastapi|uvicorn|pydantic|starlette"
CMD
fastapi 0.115.6, pydantic 2.14.3, pydantic_core 2.50.4, starlette 0.41.3, uvicorn 0.30.6
rc=0

### (tanpa timestamp tercatat) | BACKFILL: diagnosis kegagalan ETag
$ bash <<'CMD'
curl -sI "http://localhost:8000/v1/stations?limit=50" | head -8
curl -sI "http://localhost:8000/v1/stations?limit=50" | grep -ci '^etag'
CMD
HTTP/1.1 405 Method Not Allowed; allow: GET; grep menemukan 0 baris etag
rc=0 (perintah) / pipeline ETag rc=1 -> penyebab skrip mati di bawah `set -euo pipefail`
Sebab: `curl -sI` mengirim HEAD; APIRoute FastAPI tidak mendaftarkan HEAD.
Ini deviasi baru, berbeda dari deviasi #9 di README akar.

### (tanpa timestamp tercatat) | BACKFILL: baris c_etag dengan workaround GET + -D -
$ bash <<'CMD'
BASE=http://localhost:8000
FMT='%{time_namelookup},%{time_connect},%{time_appconnect},%{time_starttransfer},%{time_total},%{size_download},%{http_code},%{num_connects}\n'
ETAG=$(curl -s -D - -o /dev/null "$BASE/v1/stations?limit=50" | grep -i '^etag:' | cut -d' ' -f2 | tr -d '\r')
for _ in $(seq 10); do
  curl -o /dev/null -s -H "If-None-Match: $ETAG" -w "c_etag,$FMT" "$BASE/v1/stations?limit=50"
done
CMD
ETAG="a606db52eadb2593"; 10 baris c_etag status 304, size 0
rc=0

### (tanpa timestamp tercatat) | BACKFILL: baris d_https terhadap endpoint contoh
$ bash <<'CMD'
FMT='%{time_namelookup},%{time_connect},%{time_appconnect},%{time_starttransfer},%{time_total},%{size_download},%{http_code},%{num_connects}\n'
for _ in $(seq 10); do
  curl -s -w "d_https,$FMT" -o /dev/null https://example.com -o /dev/null https://example.com
done
CMD
20 baris d_https; koneksi_baru=1 (total ~54 ms) bergantian dengan koneksi_baru=0 (~22 ms)
rc=0
CATATAN: https://example.com adalah pengganti sementara, BUKAN endpoint dosen.

### 2026-11-10T09:35:32+07:00 | Langkah 3: uji alat ukur di device (nc + date %N)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
echo "== toybox: wget/time/date?"; "$ADB" shell 'toybox | tr " " "\n" | grep -xE "wget|time|date|nc"' < /dev/null
echo "== date %N"; "$ADB" shell 'date +%s%N' < /dev/null
echo "== satu fetch mentah via nc (unthrottled)"; "$ADB" shell '
  T0=$(date +%s%N)
  printf "GET /v1/stations?limit=500 HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 10.0.2.2\r\n\r\n" | nc 10.0.2.2 8000 | wc -c
  T1=$(date +%s%N)
  echo "elapsed_ms=$(( (T1-T0)/1000000 ))"
' < /dev/null
CMD

### 2026-11-10T09:35:39+07:00 | Langkah 3: debug nc (-w, respons mentah)
$ bash <<'CMD'

  printf "GET /v1/stations?limit=2 HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 10.0.2.2\r\n\r\n" | nc -w 3 10.0.2.2 8000 | head -c 120
  echo
  echo "rc=$?"
' < /dev/null
echo "== percobaan B: HTTP/1.1 tanpa keep-alive"; "$ADB" shell '
  printf "GET /v1/stations?limit=2 HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 10.0.2.2\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n" | nc -w 3 10.0.2.2 8000 | wc -c
' < /dev/null
CMD

' < /dev/null

### 2026-11-10T09:37:22+07:00 | Langkah 3: terapkan profil full/hsdpa/edge + jendela 2 detik
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
probe() {
  echo "-- status jaringan:"; "$ADB" emu network status < /dev/null | sed -n '1,6p'
  echo "-- byte diterima dalam jendela 2 dtk (payload penuh = 133812):"
  for i in 1 2; do
    "$ADB" shell '
      { printf "GET /v1/stations?limit=500 HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 10.0.2.2\r\n\r\n"; sleep 2; } | nc 10.0.2.2 8000 | wc -c
    ' < /dev/null
  done
}
echo "=== PROFIL full"
"$ADB" emu network speed full < /dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay none < /dev/null; probe
echo "=== PROFIL hsdpa"
"$ADB" emu network speed hsdpa < /dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay umts < /dev/null; probe
echo "=== PROFIL edge"
"$ADB" emu network speed edge < /dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay edge < /dev/null; probe

### 2026-11-10T09:37:52+07:00 | Langkah 3: waktu transfer presisi (head -c 133994 + /proc/uptime)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
timeit() {
  "$ADB" shell '
    U0=$(cut -d" " -f1 /proc/uptime | tr -d ".")
    { printf "GET /v1/stations?limit=500 HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 10.0.2.2\r\n\r\n"; sleep 20; } \
      | nc 10.0.2.2 8000 | head -c 133994 | wc -c
    U1=$(cut -d" " -f1 /proc/uptime | tr -d ".")
    echo "ms=$(( (U1-U0)*10 ))"
  ' < /dev/null
}
echo "=== full";  "$ADB" emu network speed full  < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay none < /dev/null >/dev/null; timeit; timeit
echo "=== hsdpa"; "$ADB" emu network speed hsdpa < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay umts < /dev/null >/dev/null; timeit; timeit
echo "=== edge";  "$ADB" emu network speed edge  < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay edge < /dev/null >/dev/null; timeit; timeit
CMD

### 2026-11-10T09:40:02+07:00 | Langkah 3: ukur transfer via FIFO (penulis di latar, hanya nc|head diukur)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
timeit() {
  "$ADB" shell '
    F=/data/local/tmp/req.fifo
    rm -f $F; mkfifo $F
    { printf "GET /v1/stations?limit=500 HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 10.0.2.2\r\n\r\n"; sleep 30; } > $F &
    W=$!
    U0=$(cut -d" " -f1 /proc/uptime | tr -d ".")
    nc 10.0.2.2 8000 < $F 2>/dev/null | head -c 133994 | wc -c
    U1=$(cut -d" " -f1 /proc/uptime | tr -d ".")
    kill $W 2>/dev/null; rm -f $F
    echo "ms=$(( (U1-U0)*10 ))"
  ' < /dev/null
}
echo "=== full";  "$ADB" emu network speed full  < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay none < /dev/null >/dev/null; timeit; timeit
echo "=== hsdpa"; "$ADB" emu network speed hsdpa < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay umts < /dev/null >/dev/null; timeit; timeit
echo "=== edge";  "$ADB" emu network speed edge  < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay edge < /dev/null >/dev/null; timeit; timeit
CMD
=== full
mkfifo: /data/local/tmp/req.fifo: Permission denied
0
ms=30
mkfifo: /data/local/tmp/req.fifo: Permission denied
0
ms=510
=== hsdpa
mkfifo: /data/local/tmp/req.fifo: Permission denied
0
ms=510
mkfifo: /data/local/tmp/req.fifo: Permission denied
0
ms=520
=== edge
mkfifo: /data/local/tmp/req.fifo: Permission denied
0
ms=510
mkfifo: /data/local/tmp/req.fifo: Permission denied
0
ms=520
rc=0

### 2026-11-10T09:43:42+07:00 | Langkah 3: matikan WiFi guest, ulangi uji shaper
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
echo "== status wifi sebelum"; "$ADB" shell 'cmd wifi status | head -3' < /dev/null 2>&1
echo "== matikan wifi"; "$ADB" shell 'svc wifi disable' < /dev/null; sleep 3
echo "== status wifi sesudah"; "$ADB" shell 'cmd wifi status | head -3' < /dev/null 2>&1
echo "== konektivitas radio"; "$ADB" shell 'ping -c 2 -W 2 10.0.2.2' < /dev/null 2>&1 | tail -2
timeit() {
  "$ADB" shell 'time -p sh -c "{ printf \"GET /v1/stations?limit=500 HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 10.0.2.2\r\n\r\n\"; sleep 4; } | nc 10.0.2.2 8000 | head -c 133994 | wc -c"' < /dev/null 2>&1 | tr '\n' ' '; echo
}
echo "=== full (lantai 4 dtk)";  "$ADB" emu network speed full < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay none < /dev/null >/dev/null; timeit
echo "=== edge (butuh ~2.26 dtk)"; "$ADB" emu network speed edge < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay edge < /dev/null >/dev/null; timeit

### 2026-11-10T09:46:02+07:00 | klarifikasi: emulator PUNYA internet (fetch HTTP nyata dari guest)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
fetch() {
  HOST=$1; PATHQ=$2
  "$ADB" shell "time -p sh -c '{ printf \"GET $PATHQ HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: $HOST\r\n\r\n\"; sleep 2; } | nc -w 5 $HOST 80 | wc -c'" < /dev/null 2>&1 | tr '\n' ' '; echo "  <- $HOST$PATHQ"
}
echo "== edge profile aktif:"; "$ADB" emu network speed edge < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network delay edge < /dev/null >/dev/null; "$ADB" emu network status < /dev/null | sed -n '2,5p'
echo "== fetch internet nyata dari dalam emulator (profil edge):"
fetch neverssl.com /
fetch example.com /
echo "== fetch API di laptop (10.0.2.2:8000):"
"$ADB" shell 'time -p sh -c "{ printf \"GET /v1/stations?limit=500 HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 10.0.2.2\r\n\r\n\"; sleep 0.2; } | nc 10.0.2.2 8000 | head -c 133994 | wc -c"' < /dev/null 2>&1 | tr '\n' ' '; echo "  <- 10.0.2.2:8000 (133994 B)"
echo "== kembalikan profil full"

### 2026-11-10T09:49:37+07:00 | ringkasan tabel profil + verifikasi akhir
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
python3 - <<'PY'
import csv, statistics as st, collections
rows = list(csv.DictReader(open("docs/p1_profil_jaringan.csv")))
by = collections.defaultdict(list)
for r in rows: by[(r["profil"], r["varian"])].append(float(r["waktu_dtk"]))
print(f"{'profil':7} {'varian':16} {'median ms':>10} {'maks ms':>9}")
med = {}
for k in (("full","tanpa_kompresi"),("full","kompresi"),("hsdpa","tanpa_kompresi"),
          ("hsdpa","kompresi"),("edge","tanpa_kompresi"),("edge","kompresi")):
    v = by[k]; med[k] = st.median(v)
    print(f"{k[0]:7} {k[1]:16} {st.median(v)*1000:10.1f} {max(v)*1000:9.1f}")
print()
for p in ("full","hsdpa","edge"):
    a, b = med[(p,"tanpa_kompresi")], med[(p,"kompresi")]
    print(f"{p:7} kompresi hemat {a*1000-b*1000:7.1f} ms ({(1-b/a)*100:5.1f}%)")
print()
base = med[("full","tanpa_kompresi")]
for p in ("hsdpa","edge"):
    a = med[(p,"tanpa_kompresi")]
    print(f"{p:7} vs full tanpa kompresi: {a/base:6.1f}x lebih lambat")
PY
echo "== verifikasi akhir"
echo "proses proksi: $(pgrep -cf 'throttle_proxy[.]py' || true)"
echo "port 9000   : $(ss -ltn 2>/dev/null | grep -c ':9000')"
echo "API 8000    : $(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' localhost:8000/v1/stations?limit=1)"
echo "entri log   : $(grep -c '^### ' ../logs/p1-langkah3-2026-11-10/shell-history.txt)"
echo "rc log      : $(grep -c '^rc=' ../logs/p1-langkah3-2026-11-10/shell-history.txt)"

  printf 'plugins { id("com.android.application") version "%s" apply false }\n' "$v" > build.gradle.kts

  printf 'plugins { id("com.android.application") }\nandroid { namespace = "t"; compileSdk = 36 }\n' > app/build.gradle.kts
  printf '<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"><application/></manifest>\n' > app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml

### 2026-11-10T10:00:34+07:00 | Langkah 4: tabel section 2 dari APLIKASI lewat proksi (3x tiap profil)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
OUT="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency/docs/bukti"
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
dump() { "$ADB" shell uiautomator dump /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1; "$ADB" shell cat /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null; }
tap() {
  B=$(dump | grep -o "$1[^>]*bounds=\"\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\"" | grep -oE '\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]' | head -1)
  [ -z "$B" ] && { echo "  GAGAL temukan '$1'"; return 1; }
  CX=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\1 \3/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  CY=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\2 \4/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  "$ADB" shell input tap $CX $CY < /dev/null
}
stasiun() { dump | grep -oE 'text="50 stasiun: [^"]*"' | sed -E 's/text="//; s/"$//'; }

tap "proksi :9000" || exit 1
sleep 1
for p in full hsdpa edge; do
  cd "$APP"
  nohup python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile $p > /tmp/proxy_$p.log 2>&1 &
  PID=$!
  sleep 0.8
  echo "=== profil $p | $(head -1 /tmp/proxy_$p.log)"
  for i in 1 2 3; do
    tap "Muat 50 stasiun" || exit 1
    sleep 3
    echo "  $(stasiun)"
  done
  kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true
  sleep 0.4
done
"$ADB" exec-out screencap -p < /dev/null > "$OUT/04_muat_50_proksi_edge.png"
echo "screenshot: $(ls -l "$OUT/04_muat_50_proksi_edge.png" | awk '{print $5}') bita"

### 2026-11-10T10:04:03+07:00 | Langkah 3: buktikan aplikasi benar-benar melewati proksi (log koneksi)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
dump() { "$ADB" shell uiautomator dump /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1; "$ADB" shell cat /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null; }
tap() {
  B=$(dump | grep -o "$1[^>]*bounds=\"\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\"" | grep -oE '\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]' | head -1)
  [ -z "$B" ] && { echo "  GAGAL temukan '$1'"; return 1; }
  CX=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\1 \3/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  CY=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\2 \4/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  "$ADB" shell input tap $CX $CY < /dev/null
}
echo "== base URL sekarang:"; dump | grep -oE 'text="http://[^"]*"'
cd "$APP"
python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile edge > /tmp/proxy_edge.log 2>&1 &
PID=$!; sleep 1
echo "== pendengar 9000"; ss -ltn | grep ':9000'
echo "== pid proksi $PID hidup?"; kill -0 $PID 2>/dev/null && echo ya || echo tidak
tap "proksi :9000"; sleep 0.5
tap "Muat 50 stasiun"; sleep 3
echo "== hasil aplikasi"; dump | grep -oE 'text="50 stasiun: [^"]*"'
echo "== log proksi"; cat /tmp/proxy_edge.log
kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true
CMD

$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
dump() { "$ADB" shell uiautomator dump /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1; "$ADB" shell cat /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null; }
tap() {
  B=$(dump | grep -o "$1[^>]*bounds=\"\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\"" | grep -oE '\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]' | head -1)
  [ -z "$B" ] && { echo "  GAGAL temukan '$1'"; return 1; }
  CX=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\1 \3/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  CY=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\2 \4/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  "$ADB" shell input tap $CX $CY < /dev/null
}
stasiun() { dump | grep -oE 'text="50 stasiun: [^"]*"' | sed -E 's/text="//; s/"$//'; }

if ss -ltn | grep -q ':9000'; then echo "PORT 9000 TERPAKAI, hentikan dulu"; exit 1; fi

sleep 0.5
cd "$APP"
for p in full hsdpa edge; do
  : > /tmp/proxy_$p.log
  python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile $p > /tmp/proxy_$p.log 2>&1 &
  PID=$!; sleep 1
  kill -0 $PID 2>/dev/null || { echo "proksi $p GAGAL start"; cat /tmp/proxy_$p.log; exit 1; }
  echo "=== profil $p"
  for i in 1 2 3; do
    tap "Muat 50 stasiun" || exit 1
    sleep 4
    echo "  run$i: $(stasiun)"
  done
  kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 0.4
  echo "  log proksi:"; sed 's/^/    /' /tmp/proxy_$p.log
done

### 2026-11-10T10:10:00+07:00 | Langkah 3: garis waktu OkHttp (full vs edge) dari logcat
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
dump() { "$ADB" shell uiautomator dump /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1; "$ADB" shell cat /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null; }
tap() {
  B=$(dump | grep -o "$1[^>]*bounds=\"\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\"" | grep -oE '\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]' | head -1)
  [ -z "$B" ] && { echo "  GAGAL temukan '$1'"; return 1; }
  CX=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\1 \3/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  CY=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\2 \4/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  "$ADB" shell input tap $CX $CY < /dev/null
}
stasiun() { dump | grep -oE 'text="50 stasiun: [^"]*"'; }
if ss -ltn | grep -q ':9000'; then echo "PORT 9000 TERPAKAI"; exit 1; fi
tap "proksi :9000" || exit 1
cd "$APP"
for p in full edge; do
  python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile $p > /tmp/proxy_$p.log 2>&1 &
  PID=$!; sleep 1
  "$ADB" logcat -c < /dev/null 2>/dev/null || true
  echo "=== profil $p"
  tap "Muat 50 stasiun" || exit 1
  sleep 4
  stasiun
  echo "  -- logcat:"; "$ADB" logcat -d -s MusiWatchTIMING:D < /dev/null 2>/dev/null | grep -oE "muat50.*" | sed 's/^/    /'
  kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 0.4
done

### 2026-11-10T10:11:14+07:00 | Langkah 3: uji kuantisasi 500 ms (delay 0/600/1100/1600 ms dari aplikasi)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
dump() { "$ADB" shell uiautomator dump /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1; "$ADB" shell cat /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null; }
tap() {
  B=$(dump | grep -o "$1[^>]*bounds=\"\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\"" | grep -oE '\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]' | head -1)
  [ -z "$B" ] && { echo "  GAGAL temukan '$1'"; return 1; }
  CX=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\1 \3/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  CY=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\2 \4/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  "$ADB" shell input tap $CX $CY < /dev/null
}
stasiun() { dump | grep -oE 'text="50 stasiun: [^"]*"' | sed -E 's/.*: ([0-9]+) ms.*/\1/'; }
if ss -ltn | grep -q ':9000'; then echo "PORT 9000 TERPAKAI"; exit 1; fi
cd "$APP"
for d in 0 600 1100 1600; do
  python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile full --delay-ms $d > /tmp/proxy_d$d.log 2>&1 &
  PID=$!; sleep 1
  kill -0 $PID 2>/dev/null || { echo "gagal start d=$d"; cat /tmp/proxy_d$d.log; exit 1; }
  tap "Muat 50 stasiun" || exit 1
  sleep 5
  echo "delay ${d} ms -> aplikasi: $(stasiun) ms"
  kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 0.4
  grep -oE "permintaan \+[0-9.]+ ms, bita-pertama-balasan \+[0-9.]+ ms" /tmp/proxy_d$d.log | sed 's/^/  proksi: /'
done

### 2026-11-10T10:15:44+07:00 | Langkah 3/4: build app (identity encoding) + uji proksi per-permintaan dari host
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
export ANDROID_HOME="$HOME/Android/Sdk"; export ANDROID_SDK_ROOT="$ANDROID_HOME"

cd "$APP"
for p in full edge; do
  python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile $p > /tmp/px_host_$p.log 2>&1 &
  PID=$!; sleep 0.8
  echo "=== host curl $p"
  curl -s -o /tmp/b.json -w "  1 permintaan: %{time_total} s, %{size_download} bita\n" "http://127.0.0.1:9000/v1/stations?limit=50"
  echo "  2 permintaan berurutan (keep-alive):"
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "    #1 %{time_total} s\n" "http://127.0.0.1:9000/v1/stations?limit=50" "http://127.0.0.1:9000/v1/stations?limit=50" 2>/dev/null || true
  kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 0.3
done

sleep 4

### 2026-11-10T10:16:06+07:00 | Langkah 3: verifikasi delay per-permintaan di koneksi keep-alive (host)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
cd "$APP"
for p in full hsdpa edge; do
  python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile $p > /tmp/px_chk_$p.log 2>&1 &
  PID=$!; sleep 0.8
  echo "=== $p (dua permintaan, satu koneksi keep-alive)"
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "  #%{num_connects} connect, %{time_total} s, %{size_download} bita\n" \
    "http://127.0.0.1:9000/v1/stations?limit=50" "http://127.0.0.1:9000/v1/stations?limit=50"
  kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 0.3
  grep -oE "permintaan \+[0-9.]+ ms.*" /tmp/px_chk_$p.log | sed 's/^/  /'
done
CMD

### 2026-11-10T10:16:23+07:00 | Langkah 3: verifikasi total profil = latensi + transfer (host, keep-alive)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
cd "$APP"
for p in full hsdpa edge; do
  python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile $p > /tmp/px_v2_$p.log 2>&1 &
  PID=$!; sleep 0.8
  echo "=== $p"
  curl -s -o /dev/null -o /dev/null -w "  permintaan %{num_connects}: %{time_total} s, %{size_download} bita, mulai %{time_starttransfer} s\n" \
    "http://127.0.0.1:9000/v1/stations?limit=50" "http://127.0.0.1:9000/v1/stations?limit=50"
  kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 0.3
done

### 2026-11-10T10:16:37+07:00 | Langkah 3: sapuan final 3 profil dari aplikasi (3 tap, koneksi hangat, tanpa gzip)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
OUT="$APP/docs/p1_profil_aplikasi.csv"
dump() { "$ADB" shell uiautomator dump /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1; "$ADB" shell cat /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null; }
tap() {
  B=$(dump | grep -o "$1[^>]*bounds=\"\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\"" | grep -oE '\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]' | head -1)
  [ -z "$B" ] && { echo "  GAGAL temukan '$1'"; return 1; }
  CX=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\1 \3/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  CY=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\2 \4/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  "$ADB" shell input tap $CX $CY < /dev/null
}
stasiun() { dump | grep -oE 'text="50 stasiun: [^"]*"' | sed -E 's/text="//; s/"$//'; }
if ss -ltn | grep -q ':9000'; then echo "PORT 9000 TERPAKAI"; exit 1; fi
tap "proksi :9000" || exit 1
sleep 0.5
echo "profil,run,total_ms,ttfb_ms,pemanasan_ms,bitur,item" > "$OUT"
cd "$APP"
for p in full hsdpa edge; do
  python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile $p > /tmp/px_app_$p.log 2>&1 &
  PID=$!; sleep 1
  kill -0 $PID 2>/dev/null || { echo "gagal start $p"; cat /tmp/px_app_$p.log; exit 1; }
  echo "=== profil $p"
  for i in 1 2 3; do
    tap "Muat 50 stasiun" || exit 1
    sleep 4
    LINE=$(stasiun)
    echo "  tap$i: $LINE"
    T=$(echo "$LINE" | sed -nE 's/.*: ([0-9]+) ms \(TTFB ([0-9]+) ms\), ([0-9]+) bita, ([0-9]+) item, pemanasan ([0-9]+) ms.*/\1 \2 \3 \4 \5/p')
    set -- $T
    echo "$p,$i,$1,$2,$5,$3,$4" >> "$OUT"
  done
  kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 0.4
done
echo "== CSV"; cat "$OUT"
CMD
=== profil full
  tap1: 50 stasiun: 3 ms (TTFB 3 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 508 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:9000
  tap2: 50 stasiun: 3 ms (TTFB 3 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 504 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:9000
  tap3: 50 stasiun: 3 ms (TTFB 2 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 503 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:9000
=== profil hsdpa
  tap1: 50 stasiun: 128 ms (TTFB 120 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 503 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:9000
  tap2: 50 stasiun: 129 ms (TTFB 129 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 504 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:9000
  tap3: 50 stasiun: 129 ms (TTFB 128 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 504 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:9000
=== profil edge
  tap1: 50 stasiun: 474 ms (TTFB 473 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 503 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:9000
  tap2: 50 stasiun: 473 ms (TTFB 247 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 543 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:9000
  tap3: 50 stasiun: 473 ms (TTFB 473 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 503 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:9000
== CSV
profil,run,total_ms,ttfb_ms,pemanasan_ms,bitur,item
full,1,3,3,508,13358,50
full,2,3,3,504,13358,50
full,3,3,2,503,13358,50
hsdpa,1,128,120,503,13358,50
hsdpa,2,129,129,504,13358,50
hsdpa,3,129,128,504,13358,50

### 2026-11-10T10:18:09+07:00 | Langkah 4: ulang tangkapan layar bukti (02 langsung, 03 retry, 04 edge)
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
OUT="$APP/docs/bukti"
dump() { "$ADB" shell uiautomator dump /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1; "$ADB" shell cat /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null; }
tap() {
  B=$(dump | grep -o "$1[^>]*bounds=\"\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\"" | grep -oE '\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]' | head -1)
  [ -z "$B" ] && { echo "  GAGAL temukan '$1'"; return 1; }
  CX=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\1 \3/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  CY=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\2 \4/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  "$ADB" shell input tap $CX $CY < /dev/null
}
shot() { "$ADB" exec-out screencap -p < /dev/null > "$OUT/$1"; echo "  $1: $(stat -c%s "$OUT/$1") bita"; }
echo "== (a) langsung :8000"
tap "langsung :8000"; sleep 0.5
tap "Muat 50 stasiun"; sleep 4
dump | grep -oE 'text="50 stasiun: [^"]*"'
shot "02_muat_50_langsung.png"
echo "== (b) uji flaky 20x lewat :8000"
tap "flaky 20x"; sleep 45
dump | grep -oE 'text="(tanpa|dengan) retry:[^"]*"'
shot "03_flaky_retry_langsung.png"
echo "== (c) edge :9000"
python3 "$APP/scripts/throttle_proxy.py" --profile edge > /tmp/px_shot.log 2>&1 &
PID=$!; sleep 1
tap "proksi :9000"; sleep 0.5
tap "Muat 50 stasiun"; sleep 4
dump | grep -oE 'text="50 stasiun: [^"]*"'
shot "04_muat_50_proksi_edge.png"
kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true
CMD
== (a) langsung :8000
text="50 stasiun: 3 ms (TTFB 3 ms), 13358 bita, 50 item, pemanasan 503 ms, base=http://10.0.2.2:8000"
  02_muat_50_langsung.png: 100998 bita
== (b) uji flaky 20x lewat :8000

### 2026-11-10T10:19:58+07:00 | Langkah 5: hitung median tabel P1 dari CSV
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
cd "/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
python3 - <<'PY'
import csv, statistics as st
def load(path, key):
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(path)))
    return [r for r in rows if r["kondisi"] == key]
def med(rows, col):
    return st.median(float(r[col]) for r in rows)
for key in ("a_tanpa_kompresi", "b_kompresi", "c_etag"):
    rows = load("docs/p1_curl.csv", key)
    print(key, "n=%d" % len(rows), " ".join(f"{c}={med(rows,c)*1000:.3f}ms" for c in ("dns","conn","tls","ttfb","total")),
          f"size={st.median(float(r['size']) for r in rows):.0f}")
d = list(csv.DictReader(open("docs/p1_curl_d_contoh.csv")))
new = [r for r in d if r["koneksi_baru"] == "1"]; old = [r for r in d if r["koneksi_baru"] == "0"]
for name, rows in (("d_https_baru", new), ("d_https_dipakai_ulang", old)):
    print(name, "n=%d" % len(rows), " ".join(f"{c}={med(rows,c)*1000:.3f}ms" for c in ("dns","conn","tls","ttfb","total")))
a, b, c = load("docs/p1_curl.csv","a_tanpa_kompresi"), load("docs/p1_curl.csv","b_kompresi"), load("docs/p1_curl.csv","c_etag")
sa, sb, sc = (st.median(float(r["size"]) for r in x) for x in (a,b,c))
ta, tb, tc = (st.median(float(r["total"]) for r in x) for x in (a,b,c))
print(f"size a->b: {(sb-sa)/sa*100:.2f}%  total a->b: {(tb-ta)/ta*100:+.2f}%")
print(f"size a->c: {(sc-sa)/sa*100:.2f}%  total a->c: {(tc-ta)/ta*100:+.2f}%")
print(f"a->b selisih: {(tb-ta)*1000:+.3f} ms ; a->c selisih: {(tc-ta)*1000:+.3f} ms")
app = list(csv.DictReader(open("docs/p1_profil_aplikasi.csv")))
for p in ("full","hsdpa","edge"):
    v = [int(r["total_ms"]) for r in app if r["profil"] == p]
    print(p, "median=%d maks=%d n=%d" % (st.median(v), max(v), len(v)))
PY

### 2026-11-10T10:24:22+07:00 | Bukti 05: tangkapan layar profil hsdpa (129 ms) dari aplikasi
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
ADB="$HOME/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/adb"
APP="/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
dump() { "$ADB" shell uiautomator dump /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1; "$ADB" shell cat /sdcard/ui.xml < /dev/null; }
tap() {
  B=$(dump | grep -o "$1[^>]*bounds=\"\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\[[0-9]*,[0-9]*\]\"" | grep -oE '\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]\[[0-9]+,[0-9]+\]' | head -1)
  [ -z "$B" ] && { echo "  GAGAL temukan '$1'"; return 1; }
  CX=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\1 \3/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  CY=$(echo "$B" | sed -E 's/\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]\[([0-9]+),([0-9]+)\]/\2 \4/' | awk '{print int(($1+$2)/2)}')
  "$ADB" shell input tap $CX $CY < /dev/null
}
echo "== API 8000"; curl -s -o /dev/null -w "  %{http_code}\n" http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1/stations?limit=1
echo "== 9000 bebas?"; ss -ltn | grep ':9000' || echo "  (bebas)"
echo "== restart aplikasi (layar bersih)"
"$ADB" shell am force-stop id.unsri.musiwatch < /dev/null
"$ADB" shell am start -n id.unsri.musiwatch/.MainActivity < /dev/null 2>&1 | tail -1
sleep 5
dump | grep -oE 'text="(Belum dijalankan\.|http://[^"]*)"' | sed 's/^/  /'
cd "$APP"
python3 scripts/throttle_proxy.py --profile hsdpa > /tmp/px_bukti_hsdpa.log 2>&1 &
PID=$!; sleep 1
kill -0 $PID 2>/dev/null || { echo "proksi gagal start"; cat /tmp/px_bukti_hsdpa.log; exit 1; }
tap "proksi :9000" || exit 1
sleep 0.6
echo "== base URL:"; dump | grep -oE 'text="http://[^"]*"' | sed 's/^/  /'
tap "Muat 50 stasiun" || exit 1
sleep 4
echo "== hasil:"; dump | grep -oE 'text="50 stasiun: [^"]*"' | sed 's/^/  /'
"$ADB" exec-out screencap -p < /dev/null > "$APP/docs/bukti/05_muat_50_proksi_hsdpa.png"
echo "  berkas: $(stat -c%s "$APP/docs/bukti/05_muat_50_proksi_hsdpa.png") bita"
kill $PID 2>/dev/null || true; wait $PID 2>/dev/null || true
echo "== log proksi:"; sed 's/^/  /' /tmp/px_bukti_hsdpa.log

$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u

### 2026-11-10T10:31:14+07:00 | Verifikasi penutup P1: bukti 01-06, rujukan dokumen, placeholder NIM, status git
$ bash <<'CMD'
set -u
cd "/home/derry/cloud 081126/musiwatch-praktikum/p1-api-latency"
echo "== berkas bukti vs CSV"
python3 - <<'PY'
import csv, hashlib, os
rows = list(csv.DictReader(open("docs/p1_bukti.csv")))
ok = True
for r in rows:
    p = os.path.join("docs/bukti", r["file"])
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        print(f"  HILANG {p}"); ok = False; continue
    h = hashlib.sha256(open(p, "rb").read()).hexdigest()[:16]
    size = os.path.getsize(p)
    match = "OK" if (h == r["sha256_16"] and str(size) == r["ukuran_bita"]) else "BEDA"
    if match != "OK": ok = False
    print(f"  {match:4s} {r['file']:34s} {r['latar']}")
disk = sorted(os.listdir("docs/bukti"))
csvf = sorted(r["file"] for r in rows)
print("  berkas di disk tapi tidak di CSV:", [f for f in disk if f not in csvf] or "tidak ada")
print("  di CSV tapi tidak ada di disk:", [f for f in csvf if f not in disk] or "tidak ada")
print("  HASIL:", "semua cocok" if ok else "ADA YANG TIDAK COCOK")
PY
echo
echo "== rujukan '01-05' / '01-04' yang tertinggal"
grep -rn "01-05\|01-04" README.md docs/*.md || echo "  (tidak ada)"
echo
echo "== placeholder & identitas"
grep -n "Nama/NIM" docs/P1.md
echo
echo "== status git"
git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree 2>/dev/null && git status --short | head || echo "  (belum ada repo git - sesuai instruksi, tidak di-commit)"
CMD

== README induk (20 baris pertama)
  # Kode Praktikum FSK4110 - MusiWatch
  
  Kode dari Modul Praktikum FSK4110, dipisah per praktik. Setiap folder adalah **keadaan repositori di akhir praktik itu**
  (solusi referensi kumulatif): `p2-container` = isi `p1` + tambahan P2, dan seterusnya. Untuk melihat apa yang
  berubah antarpraktik: `diff -ru p3-sync p4a-security`.
  
  | Folder | Praktik | Tag Git mahasiswa |
  | --- | --- | --- |
  | p1-api-latency | P1 - API, latency, timeout, retry (Mg 3) | p1-final |
  | p2-container | P2 - Docker, Compose, Kubernetes lokal (Mg 5) | p2-final |
  | p3-sync | P3 - Backend sinkronisasi offline-first (Mg 6-7) | p3-final |
  | p4a-security | P4a - JWT, otorisasi objek, TLS, secret (Mg 10) | p4a-final |
  | p4b-observability | P4b - Log, metrik, uji beban k6 (Mg 12) | p4b-final |
  | p5-cicd | P5 - GitHub Actions, staging kind, rollback (Mg 14) | p5-final |
  
  Setiap folder memuat `docs/Pn.md` (lembar kerja kosong sesuai pertanyaan modul).
  
  ## Penanda di dalam file
  
  - `Sumber: Modul Praktikum Pn, Langkah m (verbatim)`: sama dengan teks modul.

Praktikum FSK4110 MusiWatch: P1 selesai + kerangka P2-P5

- p1-api-latency: API FastAPI, pengukuran curl, proksi throttle,
  aplikasi Android OkHttp + retry; lembar kerja dan 6 bukti tangkapan layar
